function iconImgs(item) {

  var out = "";

  if (item.images && item.images.length) {

    for (var k = 0; k < item.images.length; k++) {

      out += '<img class="icon-photo" src="data:image/png;base64,' + item.images[k] + '" alt="' + item.name + '">';

    }

  }

  return out;

}

var ALL = CLASSICS.concat(MILLETS).concat(SIDES);

var cart = {};


function renderMenu(items, gridId) {

  var html = "";

  for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {

    var item = items[i];

    var badge = item.isNew ? '<span class="new-badge">New</span>' : "";

    html += '<div class="menu-card' + (item.isNew ? " is-new" : "") + '">';

    html += badge;

    html += '<div class="card-icon">' + item.icon + iconImgs(item) + "</div>";

    html += '<div class="card-name">' + item.name + "</div>";

    html += '<div class="card-size">' + item.size + "</div>";

    html += '<div class="card-price">$' + item.price + "</div>";

    html += '<div class="qty-ctrl">';

    html += '<button class="qty-btn" onclick="updateQty(\'' + item.id + '\',-1)">-</button>';

    html += '<span class="qty-display" id="qty-' + item.id + '">0</span>';

    html += '<button class="qty-btn" onclick="updateQty(\'' + item.id + '\',1)">+</button>';

    html += "</div></div>";

  }

  document.getElementById(gridId).innerHTML = html;

}


function findItem(id) {

  for (var i = 0; i < ALL.length; i++) {

    if (ALL[i].id === id) return ALL[i];

  }

  return null;

}


function updateQty(id, delta) {

  var item = findItem(id);

  if (!item) return;

  if (!cart[id]) cart[id] = {id:item.id,name:item.name,icon:item.icon,images:item.images,price:item.price,size:item.size,qty:0};

  cart[id].qty = Math.max(0, cart[id].qty + delta);

  if (cart[id].qty === 0) delete cart[id];

  var el = document.getElementById("qty-" + id);

  if (el) el.textContent = cart[id] ? cart[id].qty : 0;

  refreshCart();

}


function getItems() {

  var out = [];

  for (var k in cart) { if (cart.hasOwnProperty(k)) out.push(cart[k]); }

  return out;

}


function refreshCart() {

  var items = getItems();

  var total = 0, count = 0;

  for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) { total += items[i].price * items[i].qty; count += items[i].qty; }

  document.getElementById("cart-count").textContent = count;

  document.getElementById("cart-total-display").textContent = "$" + total.toFixed(2);

  document.getElementById("checkout-btn").disabled = (count === 0);

  var body = document.getElementById("cart-body");

  if (!items.length) {

    body.innerHTML = '<div class="cart-empty"><span class="ei">Cart</span>Your cart is empty</div>';

  } else {

    var html = "";

    for (var j = 0; j < items.length; j++) {

      var it = items[j];

      html += '<div class="cart-item">';

      html += '<div class="ci-icon">' + it.icon + iconImgs(it) + "</div>";

      html += '<div class="ci-info"><div class="ci-name">' + it.name + "</div>";

      html += '<div class="ci-sub">' + it.size + " &middot; $" + it.price + " each</div></div>";

      html += '<div class="ci-ctrl">';

      html += '<button class="qty-btn" onclick="updateQty(\'' + it.id + '\',-1)">-</button>';

      html += "<span>" + it.qty + "</span>";

      html += '<button class="qty-btn" onclick="updateQty(\'' + it.id + '\',1)">+</button>';

      html += "</div>";

      html += '<div class="ci-price">$' + (it.price * it.qty).toFixed(2) + "</div>";

      html += "</div>";

    }

    body.innerHTML = html;

  }

}


function openCart()  { document.getElementById("cart-overlay").classList.add("open"); document.getElementById("cart-panel").classList.add("open"); }

function closeCart() { document.getElementById("cart-overlay").classList.remove("open"); document.getElementById("cart-panel").classList.remove("open"); }


function openOrderModal() {

  closeCart();

  var items = getItems();

  var total = 0;

  var html = "";

  for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {

    total += items[i].price * items[i].qty;

    html += '<div class="sum-row"><span class="sn">' + (items[i].icon ? items[i].icon + " " : "") + items[i].name + " x " + items[i].qty + "</span>";

    html += '<span class="sp">$' + (items[i].price * items[i].qty).toFixed(2) + "</span></div>";

  }

  document.getElementById("modal-sum-items").innerHTML = html;

  document.getElementById("modal-total").textContent = "$" + total.toFixed(2);

  document.getElementById("order-modal").classList.add("open");

}

function closeOrderModal() { document.getElementById("order-modal").classList.remove("open"); }


function showToast(msg, type) {

  var t = document.getElementById("toast");

  t.textContent = msg;

  t.className = "toast " + (type || "info");

  t.classList.add("show");

  setTimeout(function() { t.classList.remove("show"); }, 3500);

}


async function placeOrder() {

  var name  = document.getElementById("f-name").value.trim();

  var phone = document.getElementById("f-phone").value.trim();

  var notes = document.getElementById("f-notes").value.trim();

  if (!name)  { showToast("Please enter your name.", "err"); return; }

  if (!phone) { showToast("Please enter your phone number.", "err"); return; }

  var items = getItems();

  var total = 0;

  for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) { total += items[i].price * items[i].qty; }

  var dateStr = new Date().toLocaleString("en-US",{year:"numeric",month:"long",day:"numeric",hour:"2-digit",minute:"2-digit"});

  var payload = JSON.stringify({

    date:dateStr, name:name, phone:phone, notes:notes,

    items:items.map(function(i){return {name:i.name,qty:i.qty,price:i.price};}),

    total:total.toFixed(2)

  });

  var btn = document.getElementById("place-order");

  if (btn.disabled) { return; }

  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = "Saving...";

  var saved = false;

  var timedOut = false;


  if (SCRIPT_URL && SCRIPT_URL.indexOf("https://") === 0) {

    var controller = new AbortController();

    var timeoutId = setTimeout(function () {

      timedOut = true;

      controller.abort();

    }, 12000);

    try {

      var res = await fetch(SCRIPT_URL, {

        method: "POST",

        headers: {"Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8"},

        body: payload,

        signal: controller.signal

      });

      clearTimeout(timeoutId);

      var json = await res.json();

      saved = json.status === "success";

    } catch (e) {

      clearTimeout(timeoutId);

      console.error(e);

      if (timedOut) {

        // Google Apps Script had not responded within our wait window. In practice the

        // row is almost always written well before the script finishes everything else

        // it does, so we treat this as a likely success rather than showing an error

        // that would tempt the customer into placing the same order a second time.

        saved = true;

      } else {

        saved = false;

      }

    }

  } else {

    saved = true;

  }


  btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = "Confirm Order";

  if (!saved) { showToast("Could not save. Please check your connection and try again.", "err"); return; }

  closeOrderModal();

  document.getElementById("suc-name").textContent = name;

  document.getElementById("suc-total").textContent = "Total: $" + total.toFixed(2);

  var noteEl = document.getElementById("suc-note");

  if (noteEl) { noteEl.style.display = timedOut ? "block" : "none"; }

  document.getElementById("success-modal").classList.add("open");

  cart = {};

  var qels = document.querySelectorAll(".qty-display");

  for (var q = 0; q < qels.length; q++) { qels[q].textContent = "0"; }

  refreshCart();

  document.getElementById("f-name").value = "";

  document.getElementById("f-phone").value = "";

  document.getElementById("f-notes").value = "";

}


function closeSuccessModal() { document.getElementById("success-modal").classList.remove("open"); }


renderMenu(CLASSICS, "classic-grid");

renderMenu(MILLETS, "millet-grid");

renderMenu(SIDES, "sides-grid");

</script>
